mPhotoEditorView.getSource()\n                .setImageBitmap(BitmapFactory.decodeFile(originalPath));\n        mPhotoEditor.clearAllViews();\n        mPhotoEditor.undo();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Increment image count to display in textView\n     */\n    private void incrementImageCount() {\n        if (mCurrentImage < mImagesCount) {\n            setImageCount();\n            mPreviousButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n        } else if (mCurrentImage == mImagesCount) {\n            setImageCount();\n            mNextButton.setVisibility(View.INVISIBLE);\n            mPreviousButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n        } else {\n            mNextButton.setEnabled(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Decrement image count to display in textView\n     */\n    private void decrementImageCount() {\n        if (mCurrentImage > 0) {\n            setImageCount();\n            mNextButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n        } else if (mCurrentImage == 0) {\n mPreviousButton.setEnabled(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void setImageCount() {\n        String sText = "Showing " + String.valueOf(mCurrentImage + 1) + " of " + mDisplaySize;\n        mImgcount.setText(sText);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Saves Current Image with applied filter\n     */\n    private void saveCurrentImage() {\n        try {\n            File sdCard = String.format(getString(R.string.filter_file_name),\n                    String.valueOf(System.currentTimeMillis()), mFilterName);\n            File outFile = new File(dir, fileName);\n            String imagePath = outFile.getAbsolutePath();\n\n            mPhotoEditor.saveAsFile(imagePath, new PhotoEditor.OnSaveListener() {\n                @Override\n                public void onSuccess(@NonNull String imagePath) {\n                    mImagepaths.remove(mCurrentImage);\n                    mImagepaths.add(mCurrentImage, imagePath);\n   private void previous() {\n        try {\n            if (mCurrentImage - 1 >= 0) {\n                mPhotoEditorView.getSource()\n AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The bug is fixed and the project compiles (and passes checkstyle).\n\n## Root cause\n\n`ImageEditor.java` gated navigation with a flag *comparison* rather than real save state:\n\n```java\nif (mClicked == mClickedFilter) { next(); ... } else { toast(R.string.save_first); }\n```\n\n`mClicked` = "Save current pressed", `mClickedFilter` = "a filter was applied". In the reported scenario (no filter, brush drawing, then *Save current*), `saveC()` took the `mDoodleSelected` branch and never called `applyFilter()`, so `mClickedFilter` stayed `false` while `mClicked` became `true`. The flags differed → **"Save Current Image First"** toast appeared even though the image had just been saved.\n\nThe same broken predicate also caused the inverse defect: drawing *without* saving left both flags `false`, so navigation proceeded silently and the doodle was lost.\n\n## The patch (`app/src/main/java/swati4star/createpdf/activity/ImageEditor.java`)\n\n1. **Replaced the flag comparison with genuine "unsaved edits" state.** New field `mUnsavedChanges`; `nextImg()`/`previousImg()` now guard on `if (!mUnsavedChanges)`.\n2. **The flag is cleared only where a save truly happens** — inside `PhotoEditor.OnSaveListener.onSuccess()` (via the new `resetEditState()` helper), so the toast can no longer fire after a successful save. It stays set on `onFailure()`.\n3. **Edits are tracked at their source, independent of filters.** New `createPhotoEditor()` helper builds the editor *and* attaches an `OnPhotoEditorListener`; `onAddViewListener`/`onRemoveViewListener`/`onStopViewChangeListener`/`onEditTextChangeListener` mark `mUnsavedChanges = true`. This makes brush strokes count as pending changes (so they\'re no longer silently discarded), while `applyFilter()` sets the flag only for a non-`NONE` filter. All three editor-construction sites now use this helper.\n4. **Supporting consistency fixes:**\n   - `resetCurrent()` now also reverts the filter effect on the existing editor and clears the edit state (reset means nothing left to save). It reverts on the current instance rather than rebuilding, to avoid dropping active brush-drawing mode.\n   - `mDoodleSelected` is now set back to `false` when brush mode is toggled off (it was previously never cleared).\n   - `mFilterName` defaults to `PhotoFilter.NONE.name()` instead of `null`, preventing a `..._null.jpg` filename on a doodle-only save.\n\nBehavior preserved: filter applied but unsaved still shows the toast; an untouched image navigates freely; save/reset/finish flows are unchanged otherwise.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 805, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 36570, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 805, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHhKzhGWPxjiJY2jHkvX', stop_reason=None, session_id='7bd0741e-54de-4b19-a320-6c301311baa2', uuid='b961c38d-f038-4cae-a96d-263e39ac4aa7')
